path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');\nconst FIXTURES_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'tests', 'fixtures', 'openspec-diff');\nconst TEMPLATES_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'templates', 'openspec');\n\nconst OPENSPEC_BIN =\n  process.env.OSQ_OPENSPEC_BIN ?? path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'node_modules', '.bin', 'openspec');\n\nconst VERSION = (() => {\n  const result = spawnSync(OPENSPEC_BIN, ['--version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });\n  if (result.status !== 0) {\n    throw new Error(`openspec ${OPENSPEC_BIN} --version failed: ${result.stdout}${result.stderr}`);\n  }\n  return result.stdout.trim();\n})();\n\nconst LABEL = `openspec ${VERSION}`;\n\ninterface CaseConfig {\n  readonly expect: 'identical' | 'both-refuse' | 'osq-refuses';\n}\n\nfunction runOpenSpec(args: readonly string[], cwd: string) {\n  return spawnSync(OPENSPEC_BIN, args, {\n    cwd,\n    encoding: 'utf8',\n    env: { ...process.env, OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY: '0' },\n  });\n}\n\n/** Every regular file under `root`, keyed by its project-relative POSIX path. */\nasync function readTree(root: string): Promise<Map<string, string>> {\n  const tree = new Map<string, string>();\n\n  async function walk(dir: string): Promise<void> {\n    const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n    for (const entry of entries.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {\n      const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n      if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n        await walk(fullPath);\n      } else if (entry.isFile()) {\n        const key = path.relative(root, fullPath).split(path.sep).join('/');\n        tree.set(key, await fs.readFile(fullPath, 'utf8'));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  await walk(root);\n  return tree;\n}\n\nasync function assertTreesEqual(left: string, right: string, message: string): Promise<void> {\n  const leftTree = await readTree(left);\n  const rightTree = await readTree(right);\n  assert.deepEqual([...leftTree.keys()], [...rightTree.keys()], message);\n  for (const [key, content] of leftTree) {\n    assert.equal(rightTree.get(key), content, `${message}: ${key}`);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function listChanges(repo: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const changesDir = path.join(repo, 'openspec', 'changes');\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(changesDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n  return entries\n    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())\n    .map((entry) => entry.name)\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/** Copy the scaffolded schema plus the case's specs and changes into `repo`. */\nasync function scaffoldRepo(repo: string, caseDir: string): Promise<void> {\n  const openspecDir = path.join(repo, 'openspec');\n  await fs.mkdir(openspecDir, { recursive: true });\n  await fs.copyFile(path.join(TEMPLATES_DIR, 'config.yaml'), path.join(openspecDir, 'config.yaml'));\n  await fs.cp(path.join(TEMPLATES_DIR, 'schemas'), path.join(openspecDir, 'schemas'), {\n    recursive: true,\n  });\n\n  const caseSpecs = path.join(caseDir, 'specs');\n  const hasSpecs = await fs\n    .stat(caseSpecs)\n    .then(() => true)\n    .catch(() => false);\n  if (hasSpecs) {\n    await fs.cp(caseSpecs, path.join(openspecDir, 'specs'), { recursive: true });\n  }\n\n  await fs.cp(path.join(caseDir, 'changes'), path.join(openspecDir, 'changes'), {\n    recursive: true,\n  });\n}\n\nasync function listDeltaSpecFiles(caseDir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const changesDir = path.join(caseDir, 'changes');\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(changesDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n  const files: string[] = [];\n  for (const change of entries) {\n    if (!change.isDirectory()) continue;\n    const specsDir = path.join(changesDir, change.name, 'specs');\n    const caps = await fs.readdir(specsDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n    for (const cap of caps) {\n      if (cap.isDirectory()) {\n        files.push(path.join(specsDir, cap.name, 'spec.md'));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\n/** The rename targets a bare RENAMED block names but OpenSpec silently skips. */\nasync function renameTargets(caseDir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const targets: string[] = [];\n  for (const file of await listDeltaSpecFiles(caseDir)) {\n    const content = await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8');\n    for (const line of content.split('\\n')) {\n      const match = line.match(/^\\s*[-*+]?\\s*TO:\\s*(.+?)\\s*$/i);\n      if (!match) continue;\n      const value = match[1]\n        .replace(/^`+/, '')\n        .replace(/`+$/, '')\n        .replace(/^###\\s*Requirement:\\s*/i, '')\n        .trim();\n      if (value) targets.push(value);\n    }\n  }\n  return targets;\n}\n\nasync function caseNames(): Promise<string[]> {\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(FIXTURES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true });\n  return entries\n    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())\n    .map((entry) => entry.name)\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/** Every fixture case with its parsed expectation, resolved once at load. */\nconst CASES: { name: string; dir: string; config: CaseConfig }[] = await (async () => {\n  const names = await caseNames();\n  return Promise.all(\n    names.map(async (name) => {\n      const dir = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, name);\n      const config = JSON.parse(\n        await fs.readFile(path.join(dir, 'case.json'), 'utf8'),\n      ) as CaseConfig;\n      return { name, dir, config };\n    }),\n  );\n})();\n\n/** Both tools are compared against the case's original `openspec/specs`. */\nasync function assertTreeMatchesBase(\n  repo: string,\n  caseDir: string,\n  message: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const actual = await readTree(path.join(repo, 'openspec', 'specs'));\n  const caseSpecs = path.join(caseDir, 'specs');\n  const hasSpecs = await fs\n    .stat(caseSpecs)\n    .then(() => true)\n    .catch(() => false);\n  const expected = hasSpecs ? await readTree(caseSpecs) : new Map<string, string>();\n  assert.deepEqual([...actual.keys()], [...expected.keys()], message);\n  for (const [key, content] of expected) {\n    assert.equal(actual.get(key), content, `${message}: ${key}`);\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe('OpenSpec differential archive parity', () => {\n  it(`${LABEL} is reported by the installed validator`, () => {\n    assert.match(VERSION, /\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+/, `${LABEL} did not report a semantic version`);\n  });\n\n  for (const { name, dir: caseDir, config } of CASES) {\n    it(`${LABEL}: case ${name} (${config.expect})`, async () => {\n      const repoA = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `osq-diff-a-${name}-`));\n      const repoB = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `osq-diff-b-${name}-`));\n\n      try {\n        await scaffoldRepo(repoA, caseDir);\n        await scaffoldRepo(repoB, caseDir);\n        const changes = await listChanges(repoA);\n\n        if (config.expect === 'identical') {\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            const validate = runOpenSpec(\n              ['validate', id, '--type', 'change', '--strict', '--json', '--no-interactive'],\n              repoA,\n            );\n            assert.equal(\n              validate.status,\n              0,\n              `${LABEL}: validate ${id} failed:\\n${validate.stdout}${validate.stderr}`,\n            );\n          }\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            const archive = runOpenSpec(['archive', id, '--yes', '--json'], repoA);\n            assert.equal(\n              archive.status,\n              0,\n              `${LABEL}: archive ${id} failed:\\n${archive.stdout}${archive.stderr}`,\n            );\n          }\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            await applyOpenSpecDeltas(\n              repoB,\n              path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'changes', id),\n              DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n            );\n          }\n          await assertTreesEqual(\n            path.join(repoA, 'openspec', 'specs'),\n            path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'specs'),\n            `${LABEL}: ${name} specs differ`,\n          );\n          return;\n        }\n\n        if (config.expect === 'both-refuse') {\n          const id = changes[0];\n          const archive = runOpenSpec(['archive', id, '--yes', '--json'], repoA);\n          assert.notEqual(\n            archive.status,\n            0,\n            `${LABEL}: archive ${id} unexpectedly succeeded:\\n${archive.stdout}`,\n          );\n          const status = JSON.parse(archive.stdout).status ?? [];\n          assert.ok(\n            status.some((entry: { code?: string }) => entry.code === 'archive_spec_update_failed'),\n            `${LABEL}: archive ${id} did not report archive_spec_update_failed:\\n${archive.stdout}`,\n          );\n          await assert.rejects(\n            applyOpenSpecDeltas(repoB, path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'changes', id), DEFAULT_CONFIG),\n            (error: unknown) => {\n              assert.ok(\n                error instanceof DeltaMergeError,\n                `${LABEL}: expected DeltaMergeError, got ${String(error)}`,\n              );\n              return true;\n            },\n            `${LABEL}: applyOpenSpecDeltas should refuse ${name}`,\n          );\n          await assertTreeMatchesBase(repoA, caseDir, `${LABEL}: assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport fs from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport os from 'node:os';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { describe, it } from 'node:test';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { DEFAULT_CONFIG } from '../src/core/foundation/config.js';\nimport { DeltaMergeError } from '../src/core/spec/delta.js';\nimport { applyOpenSpecDeltas } from '../src/watcher/archiver.js';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');\nconst FIXTURES_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'tests', 'fixtures', 'openspec-diff');\nconst TEMPLATES_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'templates', 'openspec');\n\nconst OPENSPEC_BIN =\n  process.env.OSQ_OPENSPEC_BIN ?? path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'node_modules', '.bin', 'openspec');\n\nconst VERSION = (() => {\n  const result = spawnSync(OPENSPEC_BIN, ['--version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });\n  if (result.status !== 0) {\n    throw new Error(`openspec ${OPENSPEC_BIN} --version failed: ${result.stdout}${result.stderr}`);\n  }\n  return result.stdout.trim();\n})();\n\nconst LABEL = `openspec ${VERSION}`;\n\ninterface CaseConfig {\n  readonly expect: 'identical' | 'both-refuse' | 'osq-refuses';\n}\n\nfunction runOpenSpec(args: readonly string[], cwd: string) {\n  return spawnSync(OPENSPEC_BIN, args, {\n    cwd,\n    encoding: 'utf8',\n    env: { ...process.env, OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY: '0' },\n  });\n}\n\n/** Every regular file under `root`, keyed by its project-relative POSIX path. */\nasync function readTree(root: string): Promise<Map<string, string>> {\n  const tree = new Map<string, string>();\n\n  async function walk(dir: string): Promise<void> {\n    const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n    for (const entry of entries.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {\n      const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n      if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n        await walk(fullPath);\n      } else if (entry.isFile()) {\n        const key = path.relative(root, fullPath).split(path.sep).join('/');\n        tree.set(key, await fs.readFile(fullPath, 'utf8'));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  await walk(root);\n  return tree;\n}\n\nasync function assertTreesEqual(left: string, right: string, message: string): Promise<void> {\n  const leftTree = await readTree(left);\n  const rightTree = await readTree(right);\n  assert.deepEqual([...leftTree.keys()], [...rightTree.keys()], message);\n  for (const [key, content] of leftTree) {\n    assert.equal(rightTree.get(key), content, `${message}: ${key}`);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function listChanges(repo: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const changesDir = path.join(repo, 'openspec', 'changes');\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(changesDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n  return entries\n    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())\n    .map((entry) => entry.name)\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/** Copy the scaffolded schema plus the case's specs and changes into `repo`. */\nasync function scaffoldRepo(repo: string, caseDir: string): Promise<void> {\n  const openspecDir = path.join(repo, 'openspec');\n  await fs.mkdir(openspecDir, { recursive: true });\n  await fs.copyFile(path.join(TEMPLATES_DIR, 'config.yaml'), path.join(openspecDir, 'config.yaml'));\n  await fs.cp(path.join(TEMPLATES_DIR, 'schemas'), path.join(openspecDir, 'schemas'), {\n    recursive: true,\n  });\n\n  const caseSpecs = path.join(caseDir, 'specs');\n  const hasSpecs = await fs\n    .stat(caseSpecs)\n    .then(() => true)\n    .catch(() => false);\n  if (hasSpecs) {\n    await fs.cp(caseSpecs, path.join(openspecDir, 'specs'), { recursive: true });\n  }\n\n  await fs.cp(path.join(caseDir, 'changes'), path.join(openspecDir, 'changes'), {\n    recursive: true,\n  });\n}\n\nasync function listDeltaSpecFiles(caseDir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const changesDir = path.join(caseDir, 'changes');\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(changesDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n  const files: string[] = [];\n  for (const change of entries) {\n    if (!change.isDirectory()) continue;\n    const specsDir = path.join(changesDir, change.name, 'specs');\n    const caps = await fs.readdir(specsDir, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => []);\n    for (const cap of caps) {\n      if (cap.isDirectory()) {\n        files.push(path.join(specsDir, cap.name, 'spec.md'));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\n/** The rename targets a bare RENAMED block names but OpenSpec silently skips. */\nasync function renameTargets(caseDir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const targets: string[] = [];\n  for (const file of await listDeltaSpecFiles(caseDir)) {\n    const content = await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8');\n    for (const line of content.split('\\n')) {\n      const match = line.match(/^\\s*[-*+]?\\s*TO:\\s*(.+?)\\s*$/i);\n      if (!match) continue;\n      const value = match[1]\n        .replace(/^`+/, '')\n        .replace(/`+$/, '')\n        .replace(/^###\\s*Requirement:\\s*/i, '')\n        .trim();\n      if (value) targets.push(value);\n    }\n  }\n  return targets;\n}\n\nasync function caseNames(): Promise<string[]> {\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(FIXTURES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true });\n  return entries\n    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())\n    .map((entry) => entry.name)\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/** Every fixture case with its parsed expectation, resolved once at load. */\nconst CASES: { name: string; dir: string; config: CaseConfig }[] = await (async () => {\n  const names = await caseNames();\n  return Promise.all(\n    names.map(async (name) => {\n      const dir = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, name);\n      const config = JSON.parse(\n        await fs.readFile(path.join(dir, 'case.json'), 'utf8'),\n      ) as CaseConfig;\n      return { name, dir, config };\n    }),\n  );\n})();\n\n/** Both tools are compared against the case's original `openspec/specs`. */\nasync function assertTreeMatchesBase(\n  repo: string,\n  caseDir: string,\n  message: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const actual = await readTree(path.join(repo, 'openspec', 'specs'));\n  const caseSpecs = path.join(caseDir, 'specs');\n  const hasSpecs = await fs\n    .stat(caseSpecs)\n    .then(() => true)\n    .catch(() => false);\n  const expected = hasSpecs ? await readTree(caseSpecs) : new Map<string, string>();\n  assert.deepEqual([...actual.keys()], [...expected.keys()], message);\n  for (const [key, content] of expected) {\n    assert.equal(actual.get(key), content, `${message}: ${key}`);\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe('OpenSpec differential archive parity', () => {\n  it(`${LABEL} is reported by the installed validator`, () => {\n    assert.match(VERSION, /\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+/, `${LABEL} did not report a semantic version`);\n  });\n\n  for (const { name, dir: caseDir, config } of CASES) {\n    it(`${LABEL}: case ${name} (${config.expect})`, async () => {\n      const repoA = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `osq-diff-a-${name}-`));\n      const repoB = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `osq-diff-b-${name}-`));\n\n      try {\n        await scaffoldRepo(repoA, caseDir);\n        await scaffoldRepo(repoB, caseDir);\n        const changes = await listChanges(repoA);\n\n        if (config.expect === 'identical') {\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            const validate = runOpenSpec(\n              ['validate', id, '--type', 'change', '--strict', '--json', '--no-interactive'],\n              repoA,\n            );\n            assert.equal(\n              validate.status,\n              0,\n              `${LABEL}: validate ${id} failed:\\n${validate.stdout}${validate.stderr}`,\n            );\n          }\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            const archive = runOpenSpec(['archive', id, '--yes', '--json'], repoA);\n            assert.equal(\n              archive.status,\n              0,\n              `${LABEL}: archive ${id} failed:\\n${archive.stdout}${archive.stderr}`,\n            );\n          }\n          for (const id of changes) {\n            await applyOpenSpecDeltas(\n              repoB,\n              path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'changes', id),\n              DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n            );\n          }\n          await assertTreesEqual(\n            path.join(repoA, 'openspec', 'specs'),\n            path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'specs'),\n            `${LABEL}: ${name} specs differ`,\n          );\n          return;\n        }\n\n        if (config.expect === 'both-refuse') {\n          const id = changes[0];\n          const archive = runOpenSpec(['archive', id, '--yes', '--json'], repoA);\n          assert.notEqual(\n            archive.status,\n            0,\n            `${LABEL}: archive ${id} unexpectedly succeeded:\\n${archive.stdout}`,\n          );\n          const status = JSON.parse(archive.stdout).status ?? [];\n          assert.ok(\n            status.some((entry: { code?: string }) => entry.code === 'archive_spec_update_failed'),\n            `${LABEL}: archive ${id} did not report archive_spec_update_failed:\\n${archive.stdout}`,\n          );\n          await assert.rejects(\n            applyOpenSpecDeltas(repoB, path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'changes', id), DEFAULT_CONFIG),\n            (error: unknown) => {\n              assert.ok(\n                error instanceof DeltaMergeError,\n                `${LABEL}: expected DeltaMergeError, got ${String(error)}`,\n              );\n              return true;\n            },\n            `${LABEL}: applyOpenSpecDeltas should refuse ${name}`,\n          );\n          await assertTreeMatchesBase(repoA, caseDir, `${LABEL}: ${name} changed tool A specs`);\n          await assertTreeMatchesBase(repoB, caseDir, `${LABEL}: ${name} changed osq specs`);\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const id = changes[0];\n        await assert.rejects(\n          applyOpenSpecDeltas(repoB, path.join(repoB, 'openspec', 'changes', id), DEFAULT_CONFIG),\n          (error: unknown) => {\n            assert.ok(\n              error instanceof DeltaMergeError,\n              `${LABEL}: expected DeltaMergeError, got ${String(error)}`,\n            );\n            assert.ok(\n              error.message.includes('### Requirement:'),\n              `${LABEL}: refusal must name the expected form: ${error.message}`,\n            );\n            return true;\n          },\n          `${LABEL}: applyOpenSpecDeltas should refuse ${name}`,\n        );\n\n        const archive = runOpenSpec(['archive', id, '--yes', '--json'], repoA);\n        assert.equal(\n          archive.status,\n          0,\n          `${LABEL}: archive ${id} unexpectedly failed:\\n${archive.stdout}${archive.stderr}`,\n        );\n        const living = await readTree(path.join(repoA, 'openspec', 'specs'));\n        for (const target of await renameTargets(caseDir)) {\n          const present = [...living.values()].some((content) =>\n            content.includes(`### Requirement: ${target}`),\n          );\n          assert.equal(\n            present,\n            false,\n            `${LABEL}: ${name} applied a rename OpenSpec should skip: ${target}`,\n          );\n        }\n      } finally {\n        await fs.rm(repoA, { recursive: true, force: true });\n        await fs.rm(repoB, { recursive: true, force: true });\n      }\n    });\n  }\n});\n=====FIXTURES=====\nadded\nbare-rename\ndropped-scenario\nduplicate-added\ngiven-and\nmodified\nnew-capability\nremoved\nrenamed\nsecond-change\nverify.cjs\n=====ONE Merging every delta spec of a change folder into the living specs (ADR 026).\n\nuse super::compose::merge_living_spec;\nuse super::error::MergeError;\nuse crate::markdown::parse_delta;\nuse crate::read::SpecsError;\nuse crate::validate::change::{folders, read_text};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse std::fmt;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// One delta spec of a change and the living text it merges to.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]\npub struct MergedSpec {\n    /// The capability folder name the delta sits under.\n    pub capability: String,\n    /// The delta spec's joined path.\n    pub delta: PathBuf,\n    /// The merged living text, or none for a capability the change empties.\n    pub text: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Why merging a change's delta specs failed.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]\npub enum MergeChangeError {\n    /// A delta or living spec could not be read or is not UTF-8.\n    Read(SpecsError),\n    /// A delta could not be applied to its living spec.\n    Merge {\n        /// The delta spec's joined path.\n        path: PathBuf,\n        /// The refusal the delta makes.\n        error: MergeError,\n    },\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for MergeChangeError {\n    /// Writes the read error, or the path and the merge refusal.\n    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            Self::Read(error) => error.fmt(formatter),\n            Self::Merge { path, error } => write!(formatter, \"{}: {error}\", path.display()),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for MergeChangeError {}\n\n/// Merges each delta spec of a change folder into the living specs, writing nothing.\n///\n/// Reads the `spec.md` of each folder directly under `change_dir/specs`, folders\n/// in byte order of their names, and merges it with `merge_living_spec` into the\n/// living spec at `specs_dir/<folder>/spec.md`, or none when that is not a file.\n/// Returns one `MergedSpec` per delta spec, in that order.\n///\n/// # Errors\n///\n/// Returns `MergeChangeError::Read` when a delta or living spec cannot be read\n/// or is not UTF-8, and `MergeChangeError::Merge` for the first delta that\n/// cannot be applied.\n///\n/// Implements: rust-specs/change-merge\npub fn merge_change(\n    change_dir: &Path,\n    specs_dir: &Path,\n) -> Result<Vec<MergedSpec>, MergeChangeError> {\n    let mut merged = Vec::new();\n    for folder in folders(&change_dir.join(\"specs\")) {\n        let delta_path = folder.join(\"spec.md\");\n        if !delta_path.is_file() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let Some(capability) = folder\n            .file_name()\n            .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().into_owned())\n        else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        let delta_text = read_text(&delta_path).map_err(MergeChangeError::Read)?;\n        let delta = parse_delta(&delta_text);\n        let living = living_text(specs_dir, &capability)?;\n        let text = merge_living_spec(living.as_deref(), &capability, &delta).map_err(|error| {\n            MergeChangeError::Merge {\n                path: delta_path.clone(),\n                error,\n            }\n        })?;\n\n        merged.push(MergedSpec {\n            capability,\n            delta: delta_path,\n            text,\n        });\n    }\n    Ok(merged)\n}\n\n/// Reads the living spec of one capability, or none when it has no `spec.md`.\nfn living_text(specs_dir: &Path, capability: &str) -> Result<Option<String>, MergeChangeError> {\n    let path = specs_dir.join(capability).join(\"spec.md\");\n    if !path.is_file() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n    read_text(&path).map(Some).map_err(MergeChangeError::Read)\n}\n=== The refusal a delta merge makes, with TypeScript's `DeltaMergeError` texts.\n\nuse crate::markdown::RenameSide;\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse std::fmt;\n\n/// Why a delta cannot be merged into a base spec.\n///\n/// Carries the capability merged and, except for a malformed rename, the\n/// requirement name the refusal names. not in the expected form.\n    InvalidRename {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The malformed rename line, trimmed.\n        line: String,\n    },\n    /// A rename side has no partner.\n    UnpairedRename {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// Whether the FROM or the TO side is unpaired.\n        side: RenameSide,\n        /// The name of the unpaired side.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// An ADDED name an earlier ADDED block also has.\n    DuplicateAdded {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The duplicated requirement name.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A MODIFIED name the spec has no block for.\n    ModifiedMissing {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The missing requirement name.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A rename against no base.\n    RenameWithoutBase {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The FROM name of the first rename.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A rename whose FROM name the spec has no block for.\n    RenameSourceMissing {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The FROM name of the rename.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A rename whose TO name the spec already has.\n    RenameTargetExists {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The TO name of the rename.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A REMOVED name the spec has no block for.\n    RemovedMissing {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n        /// The missing requirement name.\n        requirement: String,\n    },\n    /// A MODIFIED block dropping scenarios the current block has.\n    ScenariosDropped {\n        /// The capability merged.\n        capability: String,\n {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792396336762,"sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","part":{"partID":"prt_153259c7b001LKczh8wcUOXOG2","sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","messageID":"msg_153257bc0001ZGgOhom8ABWtNt","type":"tool","id":"call_12a6c1292eec4cd9acb24ba3","tool":"shell","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"command":"cd crates/kernel/specs && cat tests/merging_changes_differential.rs; echo \"===FROZEN===\"; find tests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff -maxdepth 3 | sort | head -60"},"output":"//! Differential merge tests: the frozen `openspec-diff` cases.\n\nuse osq_kernel_specs::{MergeChangeError, MergedSpec, merge_change};\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// The frozen `openspec-diff` fixtures folder.\nfn fixtures() -> PathBuf {\n    Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"tests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff\")\n}\n\n/// The scratch folder each case is copied to.\nfn scratch(case: &str) -> PathBuf {\n    PathBuf::from(env!(\"CARGO_TARGET_TMPDIR\"))\n        .join(\"differential\")\n        .join(case)\n}\n\n/// Reads a fixture file as UTF-8 text.\nfn read_text(path: &Path) -> String {\n    let bytes =\n        std::fs::read(path).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!(\"reading {}: {error}\", path.display()));\n    String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!(\"utf-8 of {}: {error}\", path.display()))\n}\n\n/// Copies every file and folder under `source` into `target`.\nfn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) {\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(target).expect(\"the target folder\");\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)\n        .expect(\"the source folder\")\n        .flatten()\n    {\n        let path = entry.path();\n        let destination = target.join(entry.file_name());\n        if path.is_dir() {\n            copy_tree(&path, &destination);\n        } else {\n            std::fs::copy(&path, &destination).expect(\"a copied file\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// The folders directly under `dir`, in byte order of their names.\nfn folders(dir: &Path) -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) else {\n        return Vec::new();\n    };\n    let mut folders: Vec<PathBuf> = entries\n        .flatten()\n        .filter(|entry| entry.file_type().is_ok_and(|kind| kind.is_dir()))\n        .map(|entry| entry.path())\n        .collect();\n    folders.sort_by(|left, right| left.file_name().cmp(&right.file_name()));\n    folders\n}\n\n/// Writes each merged text, or removes a capability folder the change empties.\nfn apply(specs_root: &Path, specs: &[MergedSpec]) {\n    for spec in specs {\n        let folder = specs_root.join(&spec.capability);\n        match &spec.text {\n            Some(text) => {\n                std::fs::create_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"the capability folder\");\n Differential merge tests: the frozen `openspec-diff` cases.\n\nuse osq_kernel_specs::{MergeChangeError, MergedSpec, merge_change};\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// The frozen `openspec-diff` fixtures folder.\nfn fixtures() -> PathBuf {\n    Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"tests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff\")\n}\n\n/// The scratch folder each case is copied to.\nfn scratch(case: &str) -> PathBuf {\n    PathBuf::from(env!(\"CARGO_TARGET_TMPDIR\"))\n        .join(\"differential\")\n        .join(case)\n}\n\n/// Reads a fixture file as UTF-8 text.\nfn read_text(path: &Path) -> String {\n    let bytes =\n        std::fs::read(path).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!(\"reading {}: {error}\", path.display()));\n    String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!(\"utf-8 of {}: {error}\", path.display()))\n}\n\n/// Copies every file and folder under `source` into `target`.\nfn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) {\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(target).expect(\"the target folder\");\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)\n        .expect(\"the source folder\")\n        .flatten()\n    {\n        let path = entry.path();\n        let destination = target.join(entry.file_name());\n        if path.is_dir() {\n            copy_tree(&path, &destination);\n        } else {\n            std::fs::copy(&path, &destination).expect(\"a copied file\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// The folders directly under `dir`, in byte order of their names.\nfn folders(dir: &Path) -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) else {\n        return Vec::new();\n    };\n    let mut folders: Vec<PathBuf> = entries\n        .flatten()\n        .filter(|entry| entry.file_type().is_ok_and(|kind| kind.is_dir()))\n        .map(|entry| entry.path())\n        .collect();\n    folders.sort_by(|left, right| left.file_name().cmp(&right.file_name()));\n    folders\n}\n\n/// Writes each merged text, or removes a capability folder the change empties.\nfn apply(specs_root: &Path, specs: &[MergedSpec]) {\n    for spec in specs {\n        let folder = specs_root.join(&spec.capability);\n        match &spec.text {\n            Some(text) => {\n                std::fs::create_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"the capability folder\");\n                std::fs::write(folder.join(\"spec.md\"), text).expect(\"the merged spec\");\n            }\n            None => {\n                std::fs::remove_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"the emptied capability folder\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Every file under `root`, keyed by its `/`-joined relative path.\nfn tree(root: &Path) -> BTreeMap<String, Vec<u8>> {\n    let mut files = BTreeMap::new();\n    let mut stack = vec![root.to_path_buf()];\n    while let Some(dir) = stack.pop() {\n        let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        for entry in entries.flatten() {\n            let path = entry.path();\n            if path.is_dir() {\n                stack.push(path);\n            } else {\n                let relative = path\n                    .strip_prefix(root)\n                    .expect(\"a path under the root\")\n                    .to_string_lossy()\n                    .replace('\\\\', \"/\");\n                files.insert(relative, std::fs::read(&path).expect(\"a read file\"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    files\n}\n\n/// The message of a merge refusal the case expects.\nfn refusal_message(error: &MergeChangeError) -> String {\n    match error {\n        MergeChangeError::Merge { error, .. } => error.to_string(),\n        MergeChangeError::Read(error) => panic!(\"unexpected read error: {error}\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Covers: rust-specs/differential-cases\n#[test]\nfn differential_cases() {\n    let rows: &[(&str, bool)] = &[\n        (\"added\", true),\n        (\"given-and\", true),\n        (\"modified\", true),\n        (\"new-capability\", true),\n        (\"removed\", true),\n        (\"renamed\", true),\n        (\"second-change\", true),\n        (\"bare-rename\", false),\n        (\"dropped-scenario\", false),\n        (\"duplicate-added\", false),\n    ];\n    for (case, identical) in rows {\n        let work = scratch(case);\n        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&work);\n        copy_tree(&fixtures().join(case), &work);\n\n        let specs_root = work.join(\"work\");\n        if work.join(\"specs\").is_dir() {\n            copy_tree(&work.join(\"specs\"), &specs_root);\n        }\n        let mut refusal = None;\n        for change in folders(&work.join(\"changes\")) {\n            match merge_change(&change, &specs_root) {\n                Ok(specs) => apply(&specs_root, &specs),\n                Err(error) => {\n                    refusal = Some(refusal_message(&error));\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if *identical {\n            assert_eq!(None, refusal, \"case {case} refused\");\n            let expected = tree(&work.join(\"expected\"));\n            let actual = tree(&specs_root);\n            assert_eq!(expected, actual, \"the tree of case {case}\");\n        } else {\n            let expected = read_text(&work.join(\"error.txt\"));\n            assert_eq!(Some(expected), refusal, \"the refusal of case {case}\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n===FROZEN===\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/case.json\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/changes\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/changes/001-add\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/expected\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/expected/alpha\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/specs\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/added/specs/alpha\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/case.json\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/changes\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/changes/001-bare\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/error.txt\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/specs\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/bare-rename/specs/alpha\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/case.json\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/changes\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/changes/001-drop\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/error.txt\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/specs\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/dropped-scenario/specs/alpha\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/case.json\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/changes\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/changes/001-duplicate\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/error.txt\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/specs\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/duplicate-added/specs/alpha\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/given-and\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/given-and/case.json\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/given-and/changes\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/given-and/changes/001-rich\ntests/fixtures/merge/openspec-diff/given-and/exp A crate depends only on layers\n# below it, and a feature never depends on another feature.\n[workspace]\nresolver = \"3\"\nmembers = [\n    \"crates/core\",\n    \"crates/kernel/vcs\",\n    \"crates/kernel/storage\",\n    \"crates/kernel/specs\",\n    \"crates/kernel/linter\",\n    \"crates/kernel/runners\",\n    \"crates/kernel/harnesses\",\n    \"crates/kernel/agents\",\n    \"crates/kernel/loop\",\n    \"crates/features/plan\",\n    \"crates/features/approve\",\n    \"crates/features/run\",\n    \"crates/features/archive\",\n    \"crates/features/land\",\n    \"crates/features/inbox\",\n    \"crates/features/report\",\n    \"crates/features/metaloop\",\n    \"crates/apps/cli\",\n]\n\n[workspace.package]\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\nrust-version = \"1.85\"\nlicense = \"MIT\"\npublish = false\n\n# Shared dependency versions (ADR 023). A crate names one with\n# `<name>.workspace = true` and never pins its own version.\n[workspace.dependencies]\nosq-core = { path = \"crates/core\" }\nosq-kernel-vcs = { path = \"crates/kernel/vcs\" }\nosq-kernel-storage = { path = \"crates/kernel/storage\" }\nosq-kernel-specs = { path = \"crates/kernel/specs\" }\nosq-kernel-linter = { path = \"crates/kernel/linter\" }\nosq-kernel-runners = { path = \"crates/kernel/runners\" }\nosq-kernel-harnesses = { path = \"crates/kernel/harnesses\" }\nosq-kernel-agents = { path = \"crates/kernel/agents\" }\nosq-kernel-loop = { path = \"crates/kernel/loop\" }\nosq-feature-plan = { path = \"crates/features/plan\" }\nosq-feature-approve = { path = \"crates/features/approve\" }\nosq-feature-run = { path = \"crates/features/run\" }\nosq-feature-archive = { path = \"crates/features/archive\" }\nosq-feature-land = { path = \"crates/features/land\" }\nosq-feature-inbox = { path = \"crates/features/inbox\" }\nosq-feature-report = { path = \"crates/features/report\" }\nosq-feature-metaloop = { path = \"crates/features/metaloop\" }\nserde = { version = \"1\", features = [\"derive\"] }\nserde_json = \"1\"\nrusqlite = { version = \"0.40\", features = [\"bundled\"] }\nyaml-rust2 = \"0.13\"\nsyn = { version = \"2\", features = [\"full\", \"visit\"] }\nproc-macro2 = { version = \"1\", features = [\"span-locations\"] }\nproptest = \"1\"\ninsta = \"1\"\n\n# The rulebook (ADR 023). Every crate inherits it with `[lints] workspace = true`\n# and may not loosen an entry.\n[workspace.lints.rust]\nmissing_docs = \"deny\"\nunsafe_code = \"forbid\"\n\n[workspace.lints.clippy]\npedantic = { level = \"warn\", priority = -1 }\nmissing_docs_in_private_items = \"deny\"\ntoo_many_lines = \"deny\"\n","title":"shell","metadata":{"metadata":{"status":"completed","truncated":false,"exit":0},"content":[{"type":"text","text":"# The Rust rewrite of osq, next to the TypeScript code (ADR 022).\n# Layers: apps -> features -> kernel -> core. A crate depends only on layers\n# below it, and a feature never depends on another feature.\n[workspace]\nresolver = \"3\"\nmembers = [\n    \"crates/core\",\n    \"crates/kernel/vcs\",\n    \"crates/kernel/storage\",\n    \"crates/kernel/specs\",\n    \"crates/kernel/linter\",\n    \"crates/kernel/runners\",\n    \"crates/kernel/harnesses\",\n    \"crates/kernel/agents\",\n    \"crates/kernel/loop\",\n    \"crates/features/plan\",\n    \"crates/features/approve\",\n    \"crates/features/run\",\n    \"crates/features/archive\",\n    \"crates/features/land\",\n    \"crates/features/inbox\",\n    \"crates/features/report\",\n    \"crates/features/metaloop\",\n    \"crates/apps/cli\",\n]\n\n[workspace.package]\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\nrust-version = \"1.85\"\nlicense = \"MIT\"\npublish = false\n\n# Shared dependency versions (ADR 023). A crate names one with\n# `<name>.workspace = true` and never pins its own version.\n[workspace.dependencies]\nosq-core = { path = \"crates/core\" }\nosq-kernel-vcs = { path = \"crates/kernel/vcs\" }\nosq-kernel-storage = { path = \"crates/kernel/storage\" }\nosq-kernel-specs = { path = \"crates/kernel/specs\" }\nosq-kernel-linter = { path = \"crates/kernel/linter\" }\nosq-kernel-runners = { path = \"crates/kernel/runners\" }\nosq-kernel-harnesses = { path = \"crates/kernel/harnesses\" }\nosq-kernel-agents = { path = \"crates/kernel/agents\" }\nosq-kernel-loop = { path = \"crates/kernel/loop\" }\nosq-feature-plan = { path = \"crates/features/plan\" }\nosq-feature-approve = { path = \"crates/features/approve\" }\nosq-feature-run = { path = \"crates/features/run\" }\nosq-feature-archive = { path = \"crates/features/archive\" }\nosq-feature-land = { path = \"crates/features/land\" }\nosq-feature-inbox = { path = \"crates/features/inbox\" }\nosq-feature-report = { path = \"crates/features/report\" }\nosq-feature-metaloop = { path = \"crates/features/metaloop\" }\nserde = { version = \"1\", features = [\"derive\"] }\nserde_json = \"1\"\nrusqlite = { version = \"0.40\", features = [\"bundled\"] }\nyaml-rust2 = \"0.13\"\nsyn = { version = \"2\", features = [\"full\", \"visit\"] }\nproc-macro2 = { version = \"1\", features = [\"span-locations\"] }\nproptest = \"1\"\ninsta = \"1\"\n\n# The rulebook (ADR 023). Every crate inherits it with `[lints] workspace = true`\n# and may not loosen an entry.\n[workspace.lints.rust]\nmissing_docs = \"deny\"\nunsafe_code = \"forbid\"\n\n[workspace.lints.clippy]\npedantic = { level = \"warn\", priority = -1 }\nmissing_docs_in_private_items = \"deny\"\ntoo_many_lines = \"deny\"\n"}],"providerCall":{"executed":false},"providerResult":{"executed":false},"rawInput":"{\"command\":\"cd /home/mathias/.osq/worktrees/osq/219-rust-specs-parity && sed -n '1,80p' Cargo.toml\"}"},"time":{"start":1792396380235,"end":1792396380566}}}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792396492801,"sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","part":{"partID":"prt_15327b6c1002Sm0i15bLgqHG0G","sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","messageID":"msg_15327a8bb0017iUcCRT1IPL8OX","type":"tool","id":"call_99ff0b5f92f043c4b3c738ea","tool":"write","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"path":"crates/kernel/specs/tests/openspec_archive_parity_corpus.rs","content":"//! Corpus test: the Rust merge gives the result `openspec archive` gives on\n//! every differential case of `tests/fixtures/openspec-diff/`.\n//!\n//! It runs the `OpenSpec` CLI over the cases, so it is a corpus test (rust-core\n//! \"Corpus tests\"): each test carries the corpus ignore, and only a build with\n//! the `parity` feature compiles it.\n\npub mod openspec_parity_support;\n\nuse openspec_parity_support::cli::{OpenSpecCli, text_of};\nuse openspec_parity_support::project::Project;\nuse openspec_parity_support::trees::{folders, name_of, read_tree, repository_root};\nuse osq_kernel_specs::{MergeChangeError, MergedSpec, merge_change};\nuse serde_json::Value;\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// The differential cases, relative to the repository root.\nconst FIXTURES: &str = \"tests/fixtures/openspec-diff\";\n\n/// The case folder names below, in byte order.\nconst CASES: [&str; 10] = [\n    \"added\",\n    \"bare-rename\",\n    \"dropped-scenario\",\n    \"duplicate-added\",\n    \"given-and\",\n    \"modified\",\n    \"new-capability\",\n    \"removed\",\n    \"renamed\",\n    \"second-change\",\n];\n\n/// Declares one test per differential case, each reading its `case.json`.\nmacro_rules! case_tests {\n    ($($name:ident => $case:literal),+ $(,)?) => {\n        $(\n            /// Covers: rust-specs/archive-cases-match-openspec\n            #[ignore = \"corpus test: runs the OpenSpec CLI on the differential cases\"]\n            #[test]\n            fn $name() {\n                run_case($case);\n            }\n        )+\n    };\n}\n\ncase_tests! {\n    added => \"added\",\n    bare_rename => \"bare-rename\",\n    dropped_scenario => \"dropped-scenario\",\n    duplicate_added => \"duplicate-added\",\n    given_and => \"given-and\",\n    modified => \"modified\",\n    new_capability => \"new-capability\",\n    removed => \"removed\",\n    renamed => \"renamed\",\n    second_change => \"second-change\",\n}\n\n/// Covers: rust-specs/archive-cases-match-openspec\n#[ignore = \"corpus test: runs the OpenSpec CLI on the differential cases\"]\n#[test]\nfn cases_are_exactly_the_fixture_folders() {\n    let repo = repository_root();\n    let found: Vec<String> = folders(&repo.join(FIXTURES))\n        .iter()\n        .map(|path| name_of(path))\n        .collect();\n    let declared: Vec<String> = CASES.iter().map(|name| (*name).to_string()).collect();\n    assert_eq!(found, declared, \"the differential case folders\");\n}\n\n/// Runs one case and checks the result its `case.json` names.\nfn run_case(case: &str) {\n    let repo = repository_root();\n    let dir = repo.join(FIXTURES).join(case);\n    let expect = expect_of(&dir);\n    let names = change_names(&dir);\n    let cli = OpenSpecCli::locate(&repo);\n    let cli_project = project_for(case, \"cli\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    let rust_project = project_for(case, \"rust\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    let cli_specs = cli_project.root().join(\"openspec/specs\");\n    let rust_specs = rust_project.root().join(\"openspec/specs\");\n    let base = dir.join(\"specs\");\n\n    match expect.as_str() {\n        \"identical\" => {\n            rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect(\"the Rust merge applies every change\");\n            for name in &names {\n                let validate = cli.run(\n                    &[\n                        \"validate\",\n                        name,\n                        \"--type\",\n                        \"change\",\n                        \"--strict\",\n                        \"--json\",\n                        \"--no-interactive\",\n                    ],\n                    cli_project.root(),\n                );\n                assert!(\n                    validate.status.success(),\n                    \"case {case}: validate {name} failed: {}\",\n                    text_of(&validate)\n                );\n                let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n                assert!(\n                    archive.status.success(),\n                    \"case {case}: archive {name} failed: {}\",\n                    text_of(&archive)\n                );\n            }\n            let cli_tree = tree_of(&cli_specs);\n            assert!(\n                !cli_tree.is_empty(),\n                \"case {case}: the CLI archived no living spec\"\n            );\n            assert_eq!(\n                cli_tree,\n                tree_of(&rust_specs),\n                \"case {case}: the two openspec/specs trees differ\"\n            );\n        }\n        \"both-refuse\" => {\n            let error = rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n            refusal_message(&error, case);\n            let name = first_name(&names);\n            let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n            assert!(\n                !archive.status.success(),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly succeeded: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            assert!(\n                status_codes(&archive.stdout)\n                    .iter()\n                    .any(|code| code == \"archive_spec_update_failed\"),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} did not report archive_spec_update_failed: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            let expected = tree_of(&base);\n            assert_eq!(\n                tree_of(&cli_specs),\n                expected,\n                \"case {case}: the CLI changed the specs\"\n            );\n            assert_eq!(\n                tree_of(&rust_specs),\n                expected,\n                \"case {case}: the Rust merge changed the specs\"\n            );\n        }\n        \"osq-refuses\" => {\n            let error = rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n            let message = refusal_message(&error, case);\n            assert!(\n                message.contains(\"### Requirement:\"),\n                \"case {case}: the refusal must name the expected form: {message}\"\n            );\n            let name = first_name(&names);\n            let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n            assert!(\n                archive.status.success(),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly failed: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            let living = tree_of(&cli_specs);\n            for target in rename_targets(&dir) {\n                let marker = format!(\"### Requirement: {target}\");\n                assert!(\n                    !living.values().any(|text| text.contains(&marker)),\n                    \"case {case}: OpenSpec applied a rename it should skip: {target}\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n        other => panic!(\"case {case} names an unknown expectation `{other}`\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds a temporary project from a case: the scaffolded schema, the case's\n/// specs when it has any, and each change.\nfn project_for(\n    case: &str,\n    kind: &str,\n    dir: &Path,\n    repo: &Path,\n    names: &[String],\n) -> Project {\n    let project =\n        Project::prepare(&format!(\"archive-{case}-{kind}\"), repo).expect(\"the project writes\");\n    let specs = dir.join(\"specs\");\n    let source = specs.is_dir().then_some(specs.as_path());\n    project.set_specs(source).expect(\"the living specs copy\");\n    for name in names {\n        project\n            .put_change(name, &dir.join(\"changes\").join(name))\n            .expect(\"the change copies\");\n    }\n    project\n}\n\n/// Merges each named change into the project's living specs, in order.\nfn rust_merge(project: &Project, names: &[String]) -> Result<(), MergeChangeError> {\n    let specs = project.root().join(\"openspec/specs\");\n    let changes = project.root().join(\"openspec/changes\");\n    for name in names {\n        let merged = merge_change(&changes.join(name), &specs)?;\n        apply(&specs, &merged);\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Writes each merged text, or removes a capability folder the change empties.\nfn apply(specs_root: &Path, merged: &[MergedSpec]) {\n    for spec in merged {\n        let folder = specs_root.join(&spec.capability);\n        match &spec.text {\n            Some(text) => {\n                std::fs::create_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"a capability folder\");\n                std::fs::write(folder.join(\"spec.md\"), text).expect(\"a merged spec\");\n            }\n            None => {\n                if folder.is_dir() {\n                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"an emptied folder\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Runs `archive` for one change in a CLI project.\nfn cli_archive(cli: &OpenSpecCli, project: &Project, name: &str) -> std::process::Output {\n    cli.run(&[\"archive\", name, \"--yes\", \"--json\"], project.root())\n}\n\n/// The `expect` of a case's `case.json`.\nfn expect_of(dir: &Path) -> String {\n    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(\"case.json\")).expect(\"case.json reads\");\n    let value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).expect(\"case.json parses\");\n    value\n        .get(\"expect\")\n        .and_then(Value::as_str)\n        .expect(\"case.json names expect\")\n        .to_string()\n}\n\n/// The change folder names of a case, in byte order.\nfn change_names(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {\n    folders(&dir.join(\"changes\"))\n        .iter()\n        .map(|path| name_of(path))\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// The first change name, which every case has.\nfn first_name(names: &[String]) -> &str {\n    names.first().expect(\"a case has at least one change\")\n}\n\n/// The message of a merge refusal, panicking on any other error.\nfn refusal_message(error: &MergeChangeError, case: &str) -> String {\n    match error {\n        MergeChangeError::Merge { error, .. } => error.to_string(),\n        MergeChangeError::Read(read) => panic!(\"case {case}: unexpected read error: {read}\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// The `code` of each entry of an `archive --json` `status` array.\nfn status_codes(stdout: &[u8]) -> Vec<String> {\n    let Ok(value) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(stdout) else {\n        return Vec::new();\n    };\n    value\n        .get(\"status\")\n        .and_then(Value::as_array)\n        .map_or_else(Vec::new, |entries| {\n            entries\n                .iter()\n                .filter_map(|entry| entry.get(\"code\").and_then(Value::as_str))\n                .map(str::to_string)\n                .collect()\n        })\n}\n\n/// Every file under `root`, keyed by its `/`-joined relative path, or empty\n/// when the folder is missing.\nfn tree_of(root: &Path) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {\n    read_tree(root).unwrap_or_default()\n}\n\n/// The `TO:` names of a case's delta specs, as the TypeScript differential test\n/// reads them: any bulleted `TO:` line, backticks and a `### Requirement:`\n/// prefix stripped.\nfn rename_targets(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut targets = Vec::new();\n    for change in folders(&dir.join(\"changes\")) {\n        for capability in folders(&change.join(\"specs\")) {\n            let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(capability.join(\"spec.md\")) else {\n                continue;\n            };\n            targets.extend(text.lines().filter_map(to_name));\n        }\n    }\n    targets\n}\n\n/// The requirement name a delta line's `TO:` names, or none.\nfn to_name(line: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    let trimmed = line.trim_start();\n    let trimmed = trimmed\n        .strip_prefix('-')\n        .or_else(|| trimmed.strip_prefix('*'))\n        .or_else(|| trimmed.strip_prefix('+'))\n        .unwrap_or(trimmed)\n        .trim_start();\n    let lower = trimmed.to_lowercase();\n    let rest = lower.strip_prefix(\"to:\")?;\n    let rest = trimmed[trimmed.len() - rest.len()..].trim();\n    let rest = rest.trim_start_matches('`').trim_end_matches('`').trim();\n    let rest = rest\n        .strip_prefix(\"###\")\n        .map_or(rest, |tail| tail.trim_start());\n    let lower = rest.to_lowercase();\n    let name = lower.strip_prefix(\"requirement:\").map_or(rest, |_| {\n        &rest[rest.len() - (lower.len() - \"requirement:\".len())..]\n    });\n    let name = name.trim();\n    if name.is_empty() {\n        None\n    } else {\n        Some(name.to_string())\n    }\n}\n"},"output":"Created file successfully: )+\\n    };\\n}\\n\\ncase_tests! {\\n    added => \\\"added\\\",\\n    bare_rename => \\\"bare-rename\\\",\\n    dropped_scenario => \\\"dropped-scenario\\\",\\n    duplicate_added => {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792396550822,"sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","part":{"partID":"prt_15328ad0a001RBhGUZfKvbUSJy","sessionID":"ses_eacdb5a85ffeETstJhXFodxnfQ","messageID":"msg_15328a5ac001xH75mPVi55vD0G","type":"tool","id":"call_5f1f0ca64fc54939b5cd2a1c","tool":"edit","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"path":"crates/kernel/specs/tests/openspec_archive_parity_corpus.rs","oldString":"/// Runs one case and checks the result its `case.json` names.\nfn run_case(case: &str) {\n    let repo = repository_root();\n    let dir = repo.join(FIXTURES).join(case);\n    let expect = expect_of(&dir);\n    let names = change_names(&dir);\n    let cli = OpenSpecCli::locate(&repo);\n    let cli_project = project_for(case, \"cli\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    let rust_project = project_for(case, \"rust\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    let cli_specs = cli_project.root().join(\"openspec/specs\");\n    let rust_specs = rust_project.root().join(\"openspec/specs\");\n    let base = dir.join(\"specs\");\n\n    match expect.as_str() {\n        \"identical\" => {\n            rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect(\"the Rust merge applies every change\");\n            for name in &names {\n                let validate = cli.run(\n                    &[\n                        \"validate\",\n                        name,\n                        \"--type\",\n                        \"change\",\n                        \"--strict\",\n                        \"--json\",\n                        \"--no-interactive\",\n                    ],\n                    cli_project.root(),\n                );\n                assert!(\n                    validate.status.success(),\n                    \"case {case}: validate {name} failed: {}\",\n                    text_of(&validate)\n                );\n                let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n                assert!(\n                    archive.status.success(),\n                    \"case {case}: archive {name} failed: {}\",\n                    text_of(&archive)\n                );\n            }\n            let cli_tree = tree_of(&cli_specs);\n            assert!(\n                !cli_tree.is_empty(),\n                \"case {case}: the CLI archived no living spec\"\n            );\n            assert_eq!(\n                cli_tree,\n                tree_of(&rust_specs),\n                \"case {case}: the two openspec/specs trees differ\"\n            );\n        }\n        \"both-refuse\" => {\n            let error = rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n            refusal_message(&error, case);\n            let name = first_name(&names);\n            let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n            assert!(\n                !archive.status.success(),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly succeeded: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            assert!(\n                status_codes(&archive.stdout)\n                    .iter()\n                    .any(|code| code == \"archive_spec_update_failed\"),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} did not report archive_spec_update_failed: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            let expected = tree_of(&base);\n            assert_eq!(\n                tree_of(&cli_specs),\n                expected,\n                \"case {case}: the CLI changed the specs\"\n            );\n            assert_eq!(\n                tree_of(&rust_specs),\n                expected,\n                \"case {case}: the Rust merge changed the specs\"\n            );\n        }\n        \"osq-refuses\" => {\n            let error = rust_merge(&rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n            let message = refusal_message(&error, case);\n            assert!(\n                message.contains(\"### Requirement:\"),\n                \"case {case}: the refusal must name the expected form: {message}\"\n            );\n            let name = first_name(&names);\n            let archive = cli_archive(&cli, &cli_project, name);\n            assert!(\n                archive.status.success(),\n                \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly failed: {}\",\n                text_of(&archive)\n            );\n            let living = tree_of(&cli_specs);\n            for target in rename_targets(&dir) {\n                let marker = format!(\"### Requirement: {target}\");\n                assert!(\n                    !living.values().any(|text| text.contains(&marker)),\n                    \"case {case}: OpenSpec applied a rename it should skip: {target}\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n        other => panic!(\"case {case} names an unknown expectation `{other}`\"),\n    }\n}","newString":"/// Runs one case and checks the result its `case.json` names.\nfn run_case(case: &str) {\n    let repo = repository_root();\n    let dir = repo.join(FIXTURES).join(case);\n    let expect = expect_of(&dir);\n    let names = change_names(&dir);\n    let cli = OpenSpecCli::locate(&repo);\n    let cli_project = project_for(case, \"cli\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    let rust_project = project_for(case, \"rust\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n    match expect.as_str() {\n        \"identical\" => identical_case(case, &names, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n        \"both-refuse\" => both_refuse_case(case, &dir, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n        \"osq-refuses\" => osq_refuses_case(case, &dir, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n        other => panic!(\"case {case} names an unknown expectation `{other}`\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Runs an `identical` case: both tools archive every change and the two\n/// `openspec/specs` trees match.\nfn identical_case(\n    case: &str,\n    names: &[String],\n    cli: &OpenSpecCli,\n    cli_project: &Project,\n    rust_project: &Project,\n) {\n    rust_merge(rust_project, names).expect(\"the Rust merge applies every change\");\n    for name in names {\n        let validate = cli.run(\n            &[\n                \"validate\",\n                name,\n                \"--type\",\n                \"change\",\n                \"--strict\",\n                \"--json\",\n                \"--no-interactive\",\n            ],\n            cli_project.root(),\n        );\n        assert!(\n            validate.status.success(),\n            \"case {case}: validate {name} failed: {}\",\n            text_of(&validate)\n        );\n        let archive = cli_archive(cli, cli_project, name);\n        assert!(\n            archive.status.success(),\n            \"case {case}: archive {name} failed: {}\",\n            text_of(&archive)\n        );\n    }\n    let cli_tree = tree_of(&specs_of(cli_project));\n    assert!(\n        !cli_tree.is_empty(),\n        \"case {case}: the CLI archived no living spec\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        cli_tree,\n        tree_of(&specs_of(rust_project)),\n        \"case {case}: the two openspec/specs trees differ\"\n    );\n}\n\n/// Runs a `both-refuse` case: both tools refuse the first change and neither\n/// tree moves.\nfn both_refuse_case(\n    case: &str,\n    dir: &Path,\n    cli: &OpenSpecCli,\n    cli_project: &Project,\n    rust_project: &Project,\n) {\n    let names = change_names(dir);\n    let error = rust_merge(rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n    refusal_message(&error, case);\n    let name = first_name(&names);\n    let archive = cli_archive(cli, cli_project, name);\n    assert!(\n        !archive.status.success(),\n        \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly succeeded: {}\",\n        text_of(&archive)\n    );\n    assert!(\n        status_codes(&archive.stdout)\n            .iter()\n            .any(|code| code == \"archive_spec_update_failed\"),\n        \"case {case}: archive {name} did not report archive_spec_update_failed: {}\",\n        text_of(&archive)\n    );\n    let expected = tree_of(&dir.join(\"specs\"));\n    assert_eq!(\n        tree_of(&specs_of(cli_project)),\n        expected,\n        \"case {case}: the CLI changed the specs\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        tree_of(&specs_of(rust_project)),\n        expected,\n        \"case {case}: the Rust merge changed the specs\"\n    );\n}\n\n/// Runs an `osq-refuses` case: the Rust merge refuses naming the expected\n/// form, the CLI archives, and no renamed name lands.\nfn osq_refuses_case(\n    case: &str,\n    dir: &Path,\n    cli: &OpenSpecCli,\n    cli_project: &Project,\n    rust_project: &Project,\n) {\n    let names = change_names(dir);\n    let error = rust_merge(rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n    let message = refusal_message(&error, case);\n    assert!(\n        message.contains(\"### Requirement:\"),\n        \"case {case}: the refusal must name the expected form: {message}\"\n    );\n    let name = first_name(&names);\n    let archive = cli_archive(cli, cli_project, name);\n    assert!(\n        archive.status.success(),\n        \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly failed: {}\",\n        text_of(&archive)\n    );\n    let living = tree_of(&specs_of(cli_project));\n    for target in rename_targets(dir) {\n        let marker = format!(\"### Requirement: {target}\");\n        assert!(\n            !living.values().any(|text| text.contains(&marker)),\n            \"case {case}: OpenSpec applied a rename it should skip: {target}\"\n        );\n    }\n}"},"output":"Edited crates/kernel/specs/tests/openspec_archive_parity_corpus.rs (1 Corpus test: the Rust merge gives the result `openspec archive` gives on\n2: //! every differential case of `tests/fixtures/openspec-diff/`.\n3: //!\n4: //! It runs the `OpenSpec` CLI over the cases, so it is a corpus test (rust-core\n5: //! \"Corpus tests\"): each test carries the corpus ignore, and only a build with\n6: //! the `parity` feature compiles it.\n7: \n8: pub mod openspec_parity_support;\n9: \n10: use openspec_parity_support::cli::{OpenSpecCli, text_of};\n11: use openspec_parity_support::project::Project;\n12: use openspec_parity_support::trees::{folders, name_of, read_tree, repository_root};\n13: use osq_kernel_specs::{MergeChangeError, MergedSpec, merge_change};\n14: use serde_json::Value;\n15: use std::collections::BTreeMap;\n16: use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n17: \n18: /// The differential cases, relative to the repository root.\n19: const FIXTURES: &str = \"tests/fixtures/openspec-diff\";\n20: \n21: /// The case folder names below, in byte order.\n22: const CASES: [&str; 10] = [\n23:     \"added\",\n24:     \"bare-rename\",\n25:     \"dropped-scenario\",\n26:     \"duplicate-added\",\n27:     \"given-and\",\n28:     \"modified\",\n29:     \"new-capability\",\n30:     \"removed\",\n31:     \"renamed\",\n32:     \"second-change\",\n33: ];\n34: \n35: /// Declares one test per differential case, each reading its `case.json`.\n36: macro_rules! case_tests {\n37:     ($($name:ident => $case:literal),+ $(,)?) => {\n38:         $(\n39:             /// Covers: rust-specs/archive-cases-match-openspec\n40:             #[ignore = \"corpus test: runs the OpenSpec CLI on the differential cases\"]\n41:             #[test]\n42:             fn $name() {\n43:                 run_case($case);\n44:             }\n45:         )+\n46:     };\n47: }\n48: \n49: case_tests! {\n50:     added => \"added\",\n51:     bare_rename => \"bare-rename\",\n52:     dropped_scenario => \"dropped-scenario\",\n53:     duplicate_added => \"duplicate-added\",\n54:     given_and => \"given-and\",\n55:     modified => \"modified\",\n56:     new_capability => \"new-capability\",\n57:     removed => \"removed\",\n58:     renamed => \"renamed\",\n59:     second_change => \"second-change\",\n60: }\n61: \n62: /// Covers: rust-specs/archive-cases-match-openspec\n63: #[ignore = \"corpus test: runs the OpenSpec CLI on the differential cases\"]\n64: #[test]\n65: fn cases_are_exactly_the_fixture_folders() {\n66:     let repo = repository_root();\n67:     let found: Vec<String> = folders(&repo.join(FIXTURES))\n68:         .iter()\n69:         .map(|path| name_of(path))\n70:         .collect();\n71:     let declared: Vec<String> = CASES.iter().map(|name| (*name).to_string()).collect();\n72:     assert_eq!(found, declared, \"the differential case folders\");\n73: }\n74: \n75: /// Runs one case and checks the result its `case.json` names.\n76: fn run_case(case: &str) {\n77:     let repo = repository_root();\n78:     let dir = repo.join(FIXTURES).join(case);\n79:     let expect = expect_of(&dir);\n80:     let names = change_names(&dir);\n81:     let cli = OpenSpecCli::locate(&repo);\n82:     let cli_project = project_for(case, \"cli\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n83:     let rust_project = project_for(case, \"rust\", &dir, &repo, &names);\n84:     match expect.as_str() {\n85:         \"identical\" => identical_case(case, &names, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n86:         \"both-refuse\" => both_refuse_case(case, &dir, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n87:         \"osq-refuses\" => osq_refuses_case(case, &dir, &cli, &cli_project, &rust_project),\n88:         other => panic!(\"case {case} names an unknown expectation `{other}`\"),\n89:     }\n90: }\n91: \n92: /// Runs an `identical` case: both tools archive every change and the two\n93: /// `openspec/specs` trees match.\n94: fn identical_case(\n95:     case: &str,\n96:     names: &[String],\n97:     cli: &OpenSpecCli,\n98:     cli_project: &Project,\n99:     rust_project: &Project,\n100: ) {\n101:     rust_merge(rust_project, names).expect(\"the Rust merge applies every change\");\n102:     for name in names {\n103:         let validate = cli.run(\n104:             &[\n105:                 \"validate\",\n106:                 name,\n107:                 \"--type\",\n108:                 \"change\",\n109:                 \"--strict\",\n110:                 \"--json\",\n111:                 \"--no-interactive\",\n112:             ],\n113:             cli_project.root(),\n114:         );\n115:         assert!(\n116:             validate.status.success(),\n117:             \"case {case}: validate {name} failed: {}\",\n118:             text_of(&validate)\n119:         );\n120:         let archive = cli_archive(cli, cli_project, name);\n121:         assert!(\n122:             archive.status.success(),\n123:             \"case {case}: archive {name} failed: {}\",\n124:             text_of(&archive)\n125:         );\n126:     }\n127:     let cli_tree = tree_of(&specs_of(cli_project));\n128:     assert!(\n129:         !cli_tree.is_empty(),\n130:         \"case {case}: the CLI archived no living spec\"\n131:     );\n132:     assert_eq!(\n133:         cli_tree,\n134:         tree_of(&specs_of(rust_project)),\n135:         \"case {case}: the two openspec/specs trees differ\"\n136:     );\n137: }\n138: \n139: /// Runs a `both-refuse` case: both tools refuse the first change and neither\n140: /// tree moves.\n141: fn both_refuse_case(\n142:     case: &str,\n143:     dir: &Path,\n144:     cli: &OpenSpecCli,\n145:     cli_project: &Project,\n146:     rust_project: &Project,\n147: ) {\n148:     let names = change_names(dir);\n149:     let error = rust_merge(rust_project, &names).expect_err(\"the Rust merge refuses\");\n150:     refusal_message(&error, case);\n151:     let name = first_name(&names);\n152:     let archive = cli_archive(cli, cli_project, name);\n153:     assert!(\n154:         !archive.status.success(),\n155:         \"case {case}: archive {name} unexpectedly succeeded: {}\",\n156:         text_of(&archive)\n157:     );\n158:     assert!(\n159:         status_codes(&archive.stdout)\n160: apply(specs_root: &Path, merged: &[MergedSpec]) {\n245:     for spec in merged {\n246:         let folder = specs_root.join(&spec.capability);\n247:         match &spec.text {\n248:             Some(text) => {\n249:                 std::fs::create_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"a capability folder\");\n250:                 std::fs::write(folder.join(\"spec.md\"), text).expect(\"a merged spec\");\n251:             }\n252:             None => {\n253:                 if folder.is_dir() {\n254:                     std::fs::remove_dir_all(&folder).expect(\"an emptied folder\");\n255:                 }\n256:             }\n257:         }\n258:     }\n259: }\n260: \n261: /// Runs `archive` for one change in a CLI project.\n262: fn cli_archive(cli: &OpenSpecCli, project: &Project, name: The change folder names of a case, in byte order.\n278: fn change_names(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {\n279:     folders(&dir.join(\"changes\"))\n280:         .iter()\n281:         .map(|path| name_of(path))\n282:         .collect()\n283: }\n284: \n285: /// The first change name, which every case has.\n286: fn first_name(names: &[String]) -> &str {\n287:     names.first().expect(\"a case has at least one change\")\n288: }\n289: \n290: /// The message of a merge refusal, panicking on any other error.\n291: fn refusal_message(error: &MergeChangeError, case: &str) -> String {\n292: }\n296: }\n297: \n298: /// The `code` of each entry of an `archive --json` `status` array.\n299: fn status_codes(stdout: &[u8]) -> Vec<String> {\n300:     let Ok(value) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(stdout) else {\n301:         return Vec::new();\n302:     };\n303:     value\n304:         .get(\"status\")\n305:         .and_then(Value::as_array)\n306:         .map_or_else(Vec::new, |entries| {\n307:             entries\n308:                 .iter()\n309:                 .filter_map(|entry| entry.get(\"code\").and_then(Value::as_str))\n310:                 .map(str::to_string)\n311:                 .collect()\n312:         })\n313: }\n314: \n315: /// Every file under `root`, keyed by its `/`-joined relative path, or empty\n316: /// when the folder is }\n333:     }\n334:     targets\n335: }\n336: \n337: /// The requirement name a delta line's `TO:` names, or none.\n338: fn to_name(line: &str) -> Option<String> {\n339:     let trimmed = This list only shrinks: remove an entry once its file is split back\n * within the budget. 'core/spec/parser.ts',\n];\n\n/** Recursively collect authored `.ts`, `.tsx`, and `.css` files under `dir`. */\nasync function listUiSourceFiles(dir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n  const files: string[] = [];\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n      files.push(...(await listUiSourceFiles(fullPath)));\n    } else if (\n      entry.isFile() &&\n      UI_SOURCE_EXTENSIONS.has(path.extname(entry.name)) &&\n      !entry.name.endsWith('.d.ts')\n    ) {\n      files.push(fullPath);\n    }\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\ndescribe('source line budget', () => {\n  it('keeps every non-allow-listed source file at or under 250 lines', async () => {\n    const violations = await checkLineBudget(SRC_DIR, ALLOW_LIST, MAX_LINES);\n\n    assert.deepEqual(\n      violations,\n      [],\n      `Source files exceed the ${MAX_LINES}-line budget:\\n${violations.join('\\n')}`,\n    );\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('UI source line budget', () => {\n  it('keeps every authored UI source file at or under 250 lines', async () => {\n    const files source.split('\\n').length;\n      if (lineCount > MAX_LINES) {\n        violations.push(`${path.relative(ROOT, file)} has ${lineCount} lines (max ${MAX_LINES})`);\n      }\n    }\n\n
